公司簡介 
中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅，早期業
務專注於傳統砂輪，後取得鑽石碟技術專利
授權，並因應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，
切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，
砂輪事業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。
3Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻
記、KTC、MKS、MGT) 23%、DBU 32%、
SBU 45%。

主要客戶：台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導
體、英特爾，以及載板廠欣興等 
主要競爭對手：

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

中砂 (1560 TT) 
以砂為鏡，可以升良率

永豐觀點 
以銅為鏡，可以正衣冠；以砂為鏡，可以升良率。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價調升至 1,000 元：(1) 3Q26 ABU 獲利改善優於預期，帶動公司平均毛利率
升至 42.2%(+2.2pts QoQ)且具延續力，(2) 產能建置加速，推動 2027 年獲利提
升，(3) 購置三峽廠，打開 DBU 擴產瓶頸。目標價隨 2028 年 EPS 調整，由 860
元(33x 2028 EPSF)上修至 1,000 元(32x 2028 EPSF)。

ESG 評析 
中砂企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學) 
註 1：資誠永續發展服務股份有限公司僅於ＥＳＧ評鑑系統方法學建置過程中，就評分指標提供
專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。
SinoPac Securities Investment Service licenses and applies the SASB Materiality Map® 
General Issue Categories and Disclosure Topics in our work.

### Page 2

2

中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅：中砂成立於 1953 年，2005 年上市，目前資
本額約 14.6 億元，早期業務專注於傳統砂輪，後取得鑽石碟技術專利授權，並因
應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，砂輪事
業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。3Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻記、
KTC、MKS、MGT) 23%、DBU 32%、SBU 45%。中砂總部位於新北市鶯歌區，
砂輪、鑽石相關產品主要在鶯歌、樹林、湖口廠生產，而再生/測試晶圓主要在新
竹竹北、竹南廠生產，另在日本有 1 座廠房、泰國有 2 座廠房，另已在三峽新採
購 1 座廠房。客戶涵蓋半導體/記憶體製造商如台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導體、
英特爾，以及載板廠欣興等。 
3Q26 毛利率優於預期：中砂第三季稅後 EPS 3.91 元(+12.8%QoQ，+79.8%YoY)，
優於我們預期的 3.78 元，主因毛利率 42.2%季增約 2.2 個百分點高於預期，係來
自(1) 鑽石事業部營收占比及自身毛利率改善，係受到客戶惠先進製程出貨比重增
加，帶動高接產品放大，(2) ABU 產品組合改善，來自鑽石砂輪營收占比由 40%提
升至約 50%。 
加速擴產並改善獲利：我們認為 4Q26 的毛利率仍有提升空間，係考量(1) 先進製
程及 HBM 對鑽石碟產品用量增加，且公司產能由 3Q26 6.5 萬顆/月提升至 4Q26 
8 萬顆/月(原規劃 7 萬顆/月)，2nm/A14 用鑽石碟出貨量的增加將帶動 DBU 佔比
提升及獲利改善，(2) 部分測試晶圓及再生晶圓產品自 4Q26 起對所有客戶漲價約
10%，加上測試晶圓比重回落，可改善獲利率，(3) ABU 7 月漲價的效果可在 4Q26
顯現，加上鑽石砂輪、晶圓倒角砂輪、鑽石鍍膜等高階工具比重提升。另外，研究
部認為 2027 年中砂獲利成長動能仍將來自 DBU 的成長，目前公司規劃將鑽石碟
產能提升到明年底的 11 萬顆/月(+37.5%YoY)，以因應記憶體和美系 IDM 廠的擴
大採用、成熟製程客戶的產能擴增、以及晶圓代工廠先進製程積極擴產，同時預期
公司將適時與客戶們共同分擔生產成本的增加；SBU 則透過改造部分產線，並增
加表面缺陷檢測設備，來滿足客戶 HBM 對於特殊晶圓的需求。 
下一站，半導體用砂輪：由於既有樹林廠難以滿足客戶對鑽石碟中期需求，中砂於
今日董事會通過投入不超過 5.4 億新台幣於三峽購置廠房，用於擴增鑽石碟及半導
體廠用的鑽石砂輪產能，因應 CoWoS/SoIC/HBM 等先進封裝和晶背供電應用，
我們認為有望於 2028 年起貢獻營收並改善 DBU 產品組合，並隨 HPC 產品(及搭
配的記憶體)封裝規格提升和製程轉進而成長。 
再次上修預估：研究部預估中砂 2026 年全年營收 103.2 億(+26.6%YoY)，毛利率
40.4%，稅後淨利 21.9 億(+61.0%YoY)，EPS 14.60 元(原估 14.47 元)，獲利上調
主因 ABU 獲利率改善速度優於預期。2027 年營收 133.6 億(+29.5%YoY)，毛利
率 45.4%，稅後淨利 33.2 億(+51.5%YoY)，EPS 22.08 元(原估 20.26 元)。2028
年營收 174.2 億(+30.4%YoY)，毛利率 47.8%，稅後淨利 47.6 億(+43.3%YoY)，
EPS 31.65 元(原估 26.05 元)。

營運現況與分析

### Page 2

足元の経済・金融状況と注目点
1
（出所） みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
2026. 11.17
米国では物価高で実質賃金が伸び悩む一方、
カード延滞率に改善の兆し。NY連銀によれば、
延滞率の高止まりはコロナ期の与信拡大が影響
しており、今後も緩やかな改善が続くとの見立て
米家計のクレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
米国
10月の消費者物価は市場予想を上回る結果。生産
者物価も高止まり。市場は12月利上げを織り込み。
一方で、金利上昇を背景に中古住宅販売は大幅減。
根強いインフレ圧力と住宅市場の弱さが鮮明に
欧州
ユーロ圏の7～9月期GDPは前期から減速。ドイツ
は小幅プラスも、フランスはマイナス、スペインは堅
調と主要国でばらつき。ユーロ圏全体では雇用の伸
びも横ばいとなっており、景気回復力の鈍さを確認
中国
10月の輸出は高い伸びを維持。半導体や自動車な
どがけん引。一方で輸入も大幅増。生産は輸出堅調
で底堅く推移も、投資は下落幅が拡大し、消費も力
強さ欠き低迷。消費者物価も前月から伸びが鈍化

日本
9月の実質消費支出は10カ月連続のマイナス。景気
ウォッチャー調査も6カ月ぶりに悪化し、家計部門の
弱さを示唆する内容。国内企業物価は原油高などを
受けて再び加速。今後消費者物価へ波及する公算
注目点①
日本の消費は所得の伸びを下回る緩慢な回復。
一方、大型M&Aなどから企業の資金需要は旺盛
で、銀行貸出は高い伸びを維持。預金獲得競争
が激化するなか、安定的な預金調達が重要に
日本の家計は消費に慎重、企業は借入に積極的
注目点②

（前月比の3カ月移動平均、％）
（年/月）
米国：物価高で実質賃金は伸び悩むも、クレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し
 2026年10月のコアCPI（食品・エネルギーを除く）は前月比＋0.3％と市場予想（同＋0.2％）を上回る結果
―3カ月移動平均では2025年1月以来の高水準。財・サービス価格ともに上昇が続きインフレ圧力の根強さを確認
― 名目賃金の増加が物価上昇に相殺され、実質平均時給は前月比横ばい。家計の購買力は伸び悩み
 一方、クレジットカードの90日以上延滞への新規移行率は高水準ながらも小幅に低下
―7～9月期は6.87％と前年同期の7.05％を下回り、家計の信用状況には改善の兆し
3
（出所）米国労働省より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

コアCPI クレジットカード債務の新規90日以上延滞率
（出所）ニューヨーク連銀より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
物価上昇が継続 延滞率は高水準も小幅低下 信用状況悪化に一服感
注目点①米家計のクレジットカード延滞率に改善の兆し

（出所） 各国統計、CEICより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
ASEAN5：インバウンド客数 アジア：ジェット燃料価格とクラックスプレッド（週次）
（注）ジェット燃料のクラックスプレッドは、シンガポール市場のジェット燃料価格からドバイ原油
価格を差し引いて算出
（出所）LSEG、CEICより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成
 ASEAN5のインバウンド客数はインドネシアを除き総じて低調
― タイやマレーシアではASEAN発の観光客が減少。短距離不採算航路中心にLCCの減便が継続したことなどが重石に
― インドネシアは欧州や東アジア、オセアニアをはじめ、幅広い地域からの需要が底堅く、増加が継続
 ジェット燃料価格は原油高に加え、クラックスプレッドの拡大により高水準で推移。航空会社の運航コストを押し上げ
― 先行きも燃料価格の高止まりがLCCの採算を圧迫し、短距離路線の減便などを通じて域内観光需要の回復を下押しす

るリスク

日本：7～9月期実質GDPは横ばい圏の推移。消費低調の一方、設備投資が堅調に回復
 2026年7～9月期の実質GDP（1次速報値）は前期比▲0.0％（年率▲0.1％）とほぼ横ばい
― 個人消費が2四半期連続でマイナスとなったほか、輸出が一時的要因もあり小幅減となり、成長率を下押し。一方、設備
投資はAI関連需要の堅調さを背景にプラス転換し、成長率を押し上げ
―4～6月期（前期比＋0.4％）から減速し、経済の緩やかな拡大傾向が一服
 今後、2026年度後半の実質GDPは、均してみれば小幅なプラス成長と予想
― 個人消費が精彩を欠く一方で、設備投資の拡大が支えになる構図が継続。2026年度全体では＋1％弱の成長率に

日本：7月以降の原油高等を受けて輸入物価が再加速。一部の企業間取引価格にも波及の動き
14
 10月の輸入物価は、前年比＋24.8％（前月：同＋23.8％）と再加速
―7月以降の原油高再燃に加え、タンカー運賃の上昇影響もあり、石油・石炭・天然ガスが加速
―AI・データセンター需要等を背景に、半導体等の電気・電子機器や銅関連を含む金属・同製品も引き続き高い伸び率
 輸入物価の再加速は、国内企業物価の石油・化学関連製品に波及。今後、消費者向け製品の価格にも転嫁へ

― 原油高再燃等の影響が、国内企業物価の川上から川中段階の石油・化学関連製品へ速いペースで波及
― 今後、家事用消耗品などの消費者段階へと価格転嫁が進み、消費者物価の押し上げ圧力になる見込み

## Document 044: 20261019_投資週報_通膨升溫增添波動，台積財報展現韌性

Source PDF: all report pdf/20261019_投資週報_通膨升溫增添波動，台積財報展現韌性.pdf
Report source: Yuanta (投資週報)
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 1
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

任何擔保，如有錯漏或疏忽，本行及本行之任何董事或受僱人，毋須負任何法律責任。任何人因信賴此等資料而做出或改變投資決策，應審慎衡量本
身之需求及投資風險，自行承擔一切投資風險並自負盈虧，本行不負任何法律責任。本報告受中華民國著作權法保護為元大銀行所有，非經本行同
意，不得將本報告內容之一部或全部加以複製、轉載或散布。本報告所載述的意見本行可隨時予以更改或撤回，恕不另行通知。
金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 19 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國 9 月通膨升溫，Fed 年底前再升息機率仍高： 美國 9 月 CPI 年增 3.7%，高於 8 月的
3.4%；核心 CPI 年增率亦由 2.4%升至 2.6%，顯示通膨壓力不只來自能源，核心價格亦有回
升。隔日的 PPI 年增率則由 5.6%升至 6.3%，反映上游成本壓力仍強。通膨重新升溫，使 Fed
即使面對就業降溫，也難以快速轉向寬鬆，市場對年底前再度升息的預期仍高。
◼ 台積電財報創新高，AI 需求強勁但市場轉向檢視融資風險：台積電 10/15 公布其第三季淨利

達 7,631 億元、年增 69%，連續第六季刷新紀錄，第三季美元營收 467.2 億美元，並預估第
四季營收將升至 523～543 億美元；公司同時將 2026 全年營收成長預估提高至約 44%，反映
AI 晶片與先進製程需求仍極為強勁。然而，高利率環境使市場開始檢視 AI 資料中心業者的槓
桿與融資能力，CoreWeave 約 500 億美元債務引發信用疑慮，引發市場波動。
◼ 中東衝突再度推高能源風險，油價高檔加劇全球通膨壓力：胡塞武裝上週攻擊沙烏地阿美設施，
伊朗亦表示採取新的海上攻擊策略，使荷莫茲海峽與波斯灣航運風險再度升高。10/16 布蘭特
原油與西德洲原油期貨雙雙收在近三週高點。
市場展望
◼ 科技股中期趨勢料將不變，但短線波動勢將加劇：近期市場同時面臨通膨升溫、AI 資本支出疑
慮與重要科技財報公佈在即等不確定因素，短線風險偏好可能受到壓抑。在利率維持高檔及市
場評價偏高的環境下，市場短線料將隨財報結果與市場預期而出現較大波動。然而，上週台積
電與 ASML 財報結果整體優於預期，可見 AI 相關企業營運與資本支出尚未出現全面轉弱跡象，

科技產業基本面仍具一定支撐，惟後續宜更加重視企業獲利、現金流及資本支出變動及科技子
產業的表現分化現象。
◼ 中天期美債與投資級債債券兼顧息收與利率風險：美國經濟仍具韌性，加上政府與企業發債供
給維持高檔，可能使市場利率持續處於較高水準。雖然投資級債信用利差位於相對低檔，但企
業獲利、現金流及償債能力仍具支撐，加上較高的美國公債基準利率，使投資級債的利息收益
仍具吸引力。相較之下，短天期債券面臨未來利率下降後的再投資風險，長天期債券則較容易
受到公債供給增加、期限溢酬及利率波動影響，因此中天期美國公債與投資債券較能兼顧息收
與利率風險。

Quanta Computer (2382.TW)

6
Quanta Computer
Company description
Founded in 1988, Quanta Computer is a Taiwan-based original design
manufacturer (ODM) of notebook (NB) PCs and other electronic hardware. It
is the world's largest NB ODM in terms of shipments, with major customers
including the leading Taiwan, US, and Japanese electronics brands. In
addition to notebooks, Quanta is expanding into cloud-related product
development, which accounted for 30-35% of total revenues in 2022.

Investment strategy
We rate Quanta at Buy. We expect stabilizing NB shipment, solid server sales
growth amid increasing AI-related applications, as well as rising EV
component exposure to drive better margins and stronger earnings growth.
The company’s attractive cash yield should also help support the shares on
the downside in case of market volatility.
Valuation
Our target price of NT$450 is based on a 14x PE multiple on 2027E earnings.
The target multiple is in line with historical trading average since 2021, mainly

to reflect strong earnings growth trajectory brought about by servers/AI
servers and EV components. We use a P/E-based methodology as we believe
the market will be primarily focused on Quanta's earnings performance given
the dynamics of the NB/server industry.
Risks
Downside risks that could prevent the stock from reaching our target price
include: 1) slower-than-expected server/AI server demand growth; 2)
execution risks relating to margin improvement such as production yield rate

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

Update
ASMedia Technology Inc  | Asia Pacific
Inventory correction extends
into 1Q27
Inventory adjustment extends into 1Q27. Management sees improving
motherboard sell-through, but expects customer destocking to constrain shipments
in 4Q26–1Q27. AM4 exceeded 70% of chipset shipments in 3Q and should remain
dominant in 4Q, weighing on ASPs. Management indicated GM of around 50% in
4Q26–1Q27 as foundry and backend costs rise; alternative packaging/testing sources

should begin helping from 2Q27. October's stronger proprietary mix provided some
relief, but 2027 revenue guidance remains unfinalized, with 20% growth now
considered aggressive.
Recovery likely depends on execution from 2H27. ASMedia signed a new SOW
with the customer and currently sees itself as the sole supplier. Management hopes
shipments will start in 2Q27, with meaningful platform volume from 3Q27; ASPs
would be at least 50–60% above AM4. PCIe switches contributed 6–8% of revenue

after Gen4 yield and thermal issues delayed design-ins, versus a double-digit mix
target for 2027. USB4 is expected to more than double from 2026's depressed base,
although robotics remains small. New products can support recovery, but higher
development spending limits near-term operating leverage.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
ASMedia Technology Inc (5269.TW)
Base case, residual income model. Key assumptions: cost of equity of 9.8% (beta 1.3, risk-free
rate 2% and risk premium 6%), medium-term growth rate of 8.0%, payout ratio of 75% and
terminal growth rate of 5.2%.
Risks to Upside
n AMD gains market share thanks to better-than-expected performance.
n In-house designed products grow faster than expected.
n US-China tensions improve and US tariffs alleviate.

Risks to Downside
n AMD cannot get substrate supply, causing earnings downside for ASMedia.
n Intel adopts TSMC's technologies more aggressively to compete with AMD.
n Spec migration on USB 4 is slower.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

## Document 169: 261117_citi_merida

Source PDF: all report pdf/261117_citi_merida.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-11-17
Pages: 13
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Merida (9914.TW)
3Q Briefing Takeaway – New Model Cycle to Drive 2027 Recovery; SBC 
Visibility Remains Limited 
CITI'S TAKE 
We expect Merida’s core business to recover in 2027E, supported by 
accelerating new model launches and largely normalized EU inventories. 
However, we expect the recovery in 2027E to be gradual with US demand 
remaining sluggish. Meanwhile, despite improving pre-tax profits, SBC’s 
profitability remains highly uncertain. We trim our TP to NT$67 factoring in 
a lowered NTM P/E of 11x and earnings cuts. Remain Neutral as we see 
limited near-term catalysts and prefer to await clearer evidence of 
sustainable SBC profitability and stronger core earnings momentum.
Outlook: More new model launches in 2027E to drive a sales comeback — Mgmt. 
attributed softer shipments this year to deliberate reductions in existing models 
ahead of more new product launches in 2027E. Both Merida’s own brand and its 
major OEM customer, SBC, have adopted this strategy, aiming to preserve pricing 
discipline and avoid excessive discounting. Mgmt. expects the inventory 
adjustment to conclude by year-end and most new models are scheduled to begin 
shipping from Jan-27, paving the way for a sales recovery in 2027E.  
EU leads the recovery; China stabilizes while the US remains sluggish — Merida’s 
own brand grew 14% y/y in 9M26 in EU, supported by solid demand for road and 
gravel bikes. The mgmt. believe inventory normalization is largely complete in EU. 
China’s own brand sales grew 1% y/y in 9M26, better than the original expectation 
of DD% decline, helped primarily by entry and mid end bikes. Mgmt. expects another 
single digit sales growth in China in 2027E, supported by resilient commuter 
demand. The recovery of US market, on the other hand, is lagging EU and China amid 
inflationary pressure and persisting macro uncertainties.   
Still low visibility on SBC profitability —The weak bottom-line of SBC was primarily 
due to a conservative effective tax rate assumptions rather than a deterioration of 
its fundamentals. SBC’s 9M26 pre-tax profits grew 42% y/y or +20% y/y excluding 
US tariff refunds, reflecting improving operating performance and cost discipline. 
While improving pre-tax profitability is encouraging, we remain cautious on SBC’s 
near-term earnings contribution until there is a greater clarity on its normalized 
effective tax rate and the sustainability of US profitability. (continued)
n
Angela Hsu AC
+886-2-8726-9083
angela.hc.hsu@citi.com

Earnings revisions and implications — We lower our earnings estimates by 20%
for 2026E and 6% for 2027E/2028E, mainly reflecting weaker-than-expected core
sales and profitability, alongside continued uncertainty surrounding SBC’s
profitability. We cut our TP to NT$67 based on 11x 4Q26-3Q27E P/E, down from
15.5x, factoring in weaker-than-expected core business and low visibility of SBC.
While we acknowledge the potential for a stronger sales recovery in 2027E as

Merida accelerates its new model launches, we believe the recovery will be gradual
rather than a meaningful acceleration in the underlying market demand.
Meanwhile, SBC’s uncertain net profitability and lingering US market weakness
remain the key overhang.  We would prefer to stay on the sidelines, awaiting clearer
evidence of sustainable SBC net profitability and stronger earnings momentum
from the core business. Reiterate Neutral.

Bull/Bear: Merida (9914.TW)

### Page 5

Merida (9914.TW)
17 November 2026 Citi Research
5
Merida
Company description
Merida is the second-largest bike manufacturer in Taiwan. Founded in 1972,
Merida was initially involved in OEM manufacturing before it started
marketing bikes under its own Merida brand in 1986. Merida has expanded its
footprint globally through its Merida brand and three OEM brands:
Specialized (in which Merida has a 35% stake), Centurion (51% stake), and
Miyata (45%). Merida's own brand contributes around 30-40% of total

revenues. Merida has established a proprietary network of stores throughout
China, with 16 wholly owned subsidiaries and close to 2,000 retailers that sell
only Merida-branded bikes.
Investment strategy
We rate Merida shares as Neutral. Although bike inventories have normalised
in the major markets after aggressive clearance over the past 2 years, slower-
than-expected bike demand recovery continues to weigh on Merida's topline
momentum. The EU market appears to be where we should see better growth

for 2026E, while China market also turns better than expected from 2Q26; yet
with low visibility of SBC profit trend and limited positive catalysts in sight,
we expect the share price to be range bound.
Valuation
Our target price for Merida shares of NT$93 is based on a 4Q26-3Q27E P/E of
11x, a 50% discount to its historical average of 22x during pre-Covid years
(2014-2019), in view of soft order momentum as a result of slow recovery in
bike demand, a volatile margin trend, and low visibility of SBC.

Risks
Downside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade below our target
price include: 1) e-bike adoption slower than expected; 2) weaker restocking
demand; 3) lukewarm sell-through at SBC; and 4) a weaker macro.
Upside risks that could mean the Merida shares trade above our target price
include: 1) e-bike adoption faster than expected; 2) stronger restocking
demand; 3) stronger sell-through at SBC; and 4) a strong macro.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

3Q26Pre: 환율 하락에도 성장 흐름 유지 
매출액 1조 3,477억원(+7.2%YoY, 이하 YoY 생략), 영업이익 5,957억원(-6.0%, OPM 
44.2%)로 컨센서스에 부합할 것으로 전망. 3분기 원달러 평균 환율은 1,423원으로 전분기 
평균 환율 대비 약 5.2% 하락에도 불구하고 성장 흐름 지속. 
2분기 노조 파업으로 인한 영향은 3분기 생산을 진행했으며 4분기 매출에 반영될 예정으로 
연간 실적에 미치는 영향은 제한적일 것으로 예상. GSK로부터 인수한 록빌 공장도 안정적
으로 생산이 이루어지고 있으며 CAPA 여유분은 추가 수주를 진행 중으로 28~29년 이후 
완전 가동 전망. 
4Q, 6공장 건설과 폴리펩타이드 인수 
유상 증자 관련 투자 설명서 상 투자 계획에 따르면 6공장 투자는 26년부터 29년까지 진행 
될 예정으로 29년 가동 목표. 6공장은 5공장과 동일한 18만 리터 규모로 건설되며, 연내 투
자 집행을 시작함에 따라 건설 발표도 임박한 것으로 추정. 동사는 일정 수준 이상 기존 공
장 수주를 확보한 후 후속 공장 건설을 진행해오고 있어 non-binding 수주 계약의 본계약 
전환도 순차적으로 이루어질 것으로 전망. 
폴리펩타이드 인수도 현재 공개 매수를 진행 중에 있으며 연내 마무리될 것으로 예상. 
1H26 폴리펩타이드 매출은 2.37억 유로(+41.6%)로 GLP-1 agonist 등 펩타이드 의약품 
수요 증가에 따라 높은 성장 지속. 
투자 의견 Buy, 목표 주가 200만원으로 하향 
매수 의견을 유지하며 목표 주가는 기존 210만원에서 200만원으로 하향. 목표 주가 하향은 
유상 증자에 따른 EPS 희석에 기인. 글로벌 항체 CDMO 수요가 여전히 견조하며, 폴리펩
타이드 인수로 펩타이드로의 확장에 따른 추가적인 성장 기대. 주주 대상 유상 증자 영향과 
파업 우려 등이 있으나 노사 합의 시 주가 반등 전망.

盘⾯上，个股跌多涨少，沪深京三市约 4100 股飘绿，今⽇成交 1.38 万亿。截⾄收盘，沪深
两市全天成交额 13695.63 亿元，较上⼀⽇缩量 880 亿元，创出 2025 年 7 ⽉ 7 ⽇以来新低。板
块⽅⾯，证监会午间辟谣提振信⼼，午后资⾦流向 AI 硬件侧，深南电路、沪电股份、⽣益
电⼦等表现优异。家电、海运、电信板块全天强势，⻩⾦、军⼯、传媒板块抛压明显。
摘要：
11 ⽉ 18 ⽇， A 股三⼤股指早盘⾼开回落，午后探底回升，三⼤指数最终集体收红。午后 A 股科技
线突然加速，算⼒租赁、华为算⼒、 PCB 、半导体芯⽚四个⽅向⼏乎同时点⽕，推动科创 50 指
数盘中⼀度涨超 3% 。油⽓、海运、电信等板块⾛强。贵⾦属、⽣物医药、 AI 短剧等再度陷⼊调
整，其中 AI 短剧重挫领跌。
临近尾盘，市场再次出现⼤资⾦抄底的迹象。部分宽基 ETF 尾盘放量，创业板 ETF 易⽅达、沪深
300ETF 华泰柏瑞、沪深 300ETF 华夏、沪深 300ETF 易⽅达等均明显放量，其中沪深 300ETF 华

泰柏瑞上午成交 12 亿元，全天成交 76.39 亿元，其中，最后 20 分钟成交 38.34 亿元。
此外，⾯对震荡的市场，今天盘中监管部⻔就两则市场 “ ⼩作⽂ ” 集中辟谣，明确表⽰相关传⾔均
不属实，提醒投资者勿信勿传。：证监会新闻发⾔⼈ 11 ⽉ 18 ⽇明确回应，⽹传 “ 监管要求基⾦公
司上报全部产品 AI 敞⼝ ” 的信息不属实，证监会未向基⾦管理⼈提出相关数据报送要求。据资本
市场辟谣平台，近⽇，互联⽹上传播关于 “ 国常会上有⼈表态不会再频繁救市了 ” 相关信息。经核
查，该信息为谣⾔，系编造会议内容、歪曲炒作政策，严重扰乱资本市场预期，影响极其恶
劣。
港股全天低开低⾛，恒指、恒科指双双下⾏，恒指跌近 1% ，权重科⽹股多数下跌，京东业绩报
告后⼤跌超 7% 。光模块、电路板等 AI 硬件股午后亦回升，华虹宏⼒、东⼭精密、澜起科技涨
3% 。 AI ⼤模型股反弹，智谱涨近 10% 。 Anthropic 即将于周四登录纳斯达克。债市⽅⾯，国债期
货多数上涨。商品⽅⾯，国内商品期货多数下跌，贵⾦属跌幅居前，此前国际现货⻩⾦跌破

3800 美元，因美联储 12 ⽉加息预期升温。
A 股：截⾄收盘，沪指涨 0.09% ，深成指涨 0.20% ，创业板指涨 0.85% 。

### Page 5

第⼀把⽕是隔夜英伟达的财报。11 ⽉ 17 ⽇，英伟达交出 2027 财年第三季度成绩单：单季营收
1158 亿美元，同⽐增⻓ 103.1% ，历史上第⼀次单季破千亿美元；净利润 653 亿美元，同⽐增
105% ；数据中⼼营收 1076 亿美元，占总营收的 93% 。
更让市场吃下定⼼丸的是指引 —— 第四财季营收给到约 1300 亿美元，⾼于分析师预期的 1252.5
亿； 2028 财年营收增速预期从 70% 上调到 75% ，新⼀代 Vera Rubin NVL72 平台进⼊量产爬坡，
董事会还批准了 1500 亿美元的追加回购。此外，据中国基⾦报，当地时间 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇，英伟达临
时 CEO 克雷斯在财报电话会上确认，公司将停⽌向中国供应 H200 芯⽚。
第⼆把⽕是 BIS 的远程算⼒新规。11 ⽉ 16 ⽇公布的这份规则，把管制⼝径从 " 卡实物芯⽚ " 升级
到 " 卡远程算⼒访问 "—— 海外数据中⼼向国内主体提供超过 1200 万聚合 TPP 的算⼒，就得申请许

可，⼤约相当于 750 颗 H100 。 IFP 测算，国内可远程调⽤的海外算⼒约 105 万张 H100 当量， 75%
压在东南亚。这部分需求⼀旦回流，存量合规算⼒的稀缺性就要被重新定价。
第 三 把 ⽕ 来 ⾃ 华 为 昇 腾 的 具 ⾝ 智 能 叙 事 。 11 ⽉ 1 6 ⽇ 华 为 云 发 布 昇 腾 原 ⽣ 具 ⾝ 世 界 模 型
CloudRobo-WM1.2 并开源，在 RoboTwin 2.0 与 LiberoPro 2.0 两个评测基准⾥拿了榜⾸；官⽅⼝

### Page 6

径是华为昇腾云覆盖 80% 的国内具⾝智能企业、核⼼⾼校全覆盖。再叠加⼯信部 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇同意
北京、上海等 8 省市开展 6G 创新发展部省协同试点，通信与算⼒的想象空间被⼀起打开。
银河证券在最新周报⾥点得直接： " 国产算⼒链将是未来⼀段时间的主线⽅向，半导体设备、算
⼒芯⽚是国产替代攻坚的两⼤⽅向。 " 中信建投则从海外算账的⻆度给了个分⺟ ——2027 年海外
五⼤ CSP 资本开⽀在保守、乐观情景下分别达 1.12 万亿、 1.38 万亿美元，对应同⽐增速 38% 、
70% ，⾕歌、亚⻢逊及 Meta 三家采购承诺合计 1.63 万亿美元。
PCB ：尾盘上演涨停潮，供给紧张是硬逻辑
算⼒链⾥， PCB 是下午最猛的⼀⽀，⽽且尾盘还在加码。印制电路板板块涨 3.13% 。沪电股份、
深南电路尾盘双双封死涨停，⽣益电⼦盘中⼀度触及 20cm 涨停、收盘涨 18.53% ，景旺电⼦涨
7.20% ，鹏⿍控股涨 6.71% ，胜宏科技涨 6.42% ，⼯业富联涨 5.68% 。

这条线的逻辑，券商给的是 " 需求确定、供给刚性 " 的组合。花旗研报指出，下游 PCB ⼚商百亿级
扩产如⽕如荼，但上游⾼端基材受制于设备瓶颈、能耗审批与认证周期，有效产能释放严重滞
后，供给紧张格局延续。
国⾦证券则从需求端补了⼀句： AI 服务器、交换机和⾼速互联需求，正拉动⾼多层板、⾼阶 HDI
和低损耗材料放量，单机柜 PCB ⽤量和价值量都在抬升，头部⼚商⾼端产能利⽤率不低。
数据也站在业绩这⼀边。 2026 年三季度光模块及光器件板块总营收同⽐增 96% 、归⺟净利润增
137% ；胜宏科技在⼿订单处于历史⾼位，排单已经延续到 2028 年；景旺电⼦最新公告显⽰，通
信与数据基础设施收⼊占⽐超过两成， AI 服务器、⾼速光模块细分增⻓明显。
半导体芯⽚：午后拉升，代⼯涨价的故事⼜续上了

### Page 7

芯⽚⽅向是科创 50 领涨的真正推⼿。盛合晶微涨 11.20% ，华虹宏⼒涨 7.28% ，摩尔线程涨
7.28% ，伟测科技涨 5.61% ，澜起科技涨 5.51% ，寒武纪涨 4.89% ，海光信息涨 3.59% ，兆易创
新涨 2.33% ，中芯国际涨 1.45% 。
催化的核⼼是晶圆代⼯涨价。 11 ⽉ 17 ⽇，有媒体报道，转述芯⽚设计业内⼈⼠的话说，已接获
晶圆代⼯调价通知，涨幅从 10% ⾄逾 20% 不等，新成本将于 2027 年⾸季⽣效；台积电则计划
2027 年对先进及成熟制程调价，并对⾼性能计算（ HPC ）芯⽚新单在基础涨幅之上再加收 10%
⾄ 15% 的溢价。
业绩是更硬的锚。中芯国际三季度销售收⼊ 31.9 亿美元，同⽐增 33.9% 、环⽐增 6.1% ，创历史
新⾼并超指引上限，⽑利率 29.9% 环⽐提升 4.6 个百分点，产能利⽤率升⾄ 97.3% ；华虹三季度
销售收⼊ 9.871 亿美元，同⽐增 19.9% 再创历史新⾼，⽑利率 24.6% 同⽐上升 7.6 个百分点，归⺟

净利润同⽐⼤增 147.1% ，产能利⽤率⾼达 102.9% 。
招商证券认为，台积电先进制程深度受益 AI 算⼒芯⽚浪潮，成熟制程本轮景⽓更多来⾃ AI 产业链
需求及外溢效应，全球成熟制程供给缺⼝持续拉⼤；中芯国际、华虹等或上调部分品类代⼯价
格，展望 2027 年部分产线执⾏新价格后 ASP 有望继续上⾏。
贵⾦属板块下挫、紫⾦矿业跌超 5%
贵⾦属板块整体⾛低，中⾦⻩⾦跌超 7% ，紫⾦矿业、西部⻩⾦、⼭⾦国际跌超 5% ，四川⻩⾦、
⼭东⻩⾦、⾚峰⻩⾦等同步⾛低。

### Page 8

VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh 
khoản suy giảm

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy giảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

### Page 4

4

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 313: Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-19)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Cliff Notes_ whiplash (2026-11-19).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-19
Pages: 11
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: whiplash
By Ryan Wells & Elliot Clarke
11:20 November 06 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
In Australia, the RBA delivered another 25bp rate increase at its November meeting, taking the cash
rate to 4.85%. The move was widely expected, reflecting the Board’s continued concern around
inflation following the recent run higher in global energy prices, firmer domestic capacity pressures and

signs that inflation expectations have edged up. Inflation is now not expected to return to the mid-point
of the target band until mid-2028. This is despite weaker growth expectations and the unemployment

### Page 2

rate trending up to 5.1% by end-2028 on the RBA’s forecasts.
Importantly, though, the decision was not unanimous, with three members preferring to remain on hold
given downside risks to activity and the restraining influence of slower growth. Governor Bullock’s
suggestion that it was time to “take stock” after five rate increases also struck a less hawkish note,
while the statement dropped September’s explicit reference to raising rates further “if needed”. In our

view, February represents the next plausible opportunity for another hike, but we see this only as a risk.
Policy’s effect on housing is becoming increasingly visible. Cotality home prices fell a further 1.2% in
October to now be 6.7% below March’s peak. The cumulative price decline is approaching the scale of
previous housing corrections but appears to have regained momentum after September’s rate
increase. Dwelling approvals saw a 5.1% headline gain in September, but this was driven by volatile

unit approvals while detached house approvals fell sharply, suggesting higher rates and the
established-market downturn are beginning to weigh on the construction pipeline.
Official data on nominal household spending meanwhile rose 0.6% in September and 2.1% for Q3;
however, much of the recent acceleration looks to be price-driven, particularly for fuel. Real spending
volumes expanded by a much more modest (but still solid) 0.7% in Q3.
The Westpac-DataX Consumer Panel provides a granular look at income, spending and saving flows.

The latest update suggests that, beneath the resilient headline story, households are increasingly being
squeezed, particularly those with low savings buffers. With another rate increase now delivered,
housing wealth declining and sentiment weak, Q3 increasingly looks like the high-water mark for
growth in consumer spending.
Lastly for Australia, in September the goods trade surplus widened sharply to $2.8bn, with non-
monetary gold trade yet again adding considerable noise month-to-month. The most notable

development was another exceptionally strong result for data-processing equipment imports, taking the
Q3 total to $11.1bn. This is another clear marker of the scale of Australia’s data-centre investment
boom, which should feature prominently in both business investment and imports in the Q3 National
Accounts.
Offshore, the world has been focused on the US mid-term elections. In the House, Senate and a series
of State Governor races, the Democrats experienced resounding victory. Come 2027, the Democrats

will have a comfortable majority in both houses of Congress and are expected to make use of this
strength to respond to voter concerns, particularly over cost-of-living pressures. The election result also
limits President Trump’s ability to fund an extended military action against Iran. Notably, both
immediately before and after the election, President Trump and the administration have exhibited a
greater interest in furthering negotiations with Iran. It is therefore not a surprise that Brent oil has

backed off its most recent highs around USD118 last Friday, although the scale of the drop to near
USD93 yesterday is arguably too optimistic. Brent has since traded back up to above USD99, still 16%
below Friday’s peak.
While the initial reaction to the US election result was a robust broad-based rally in major equity
indexes, concerns over the potential for tighter regulation of AI and the related infrastructure buildout

### Page 3

Related articles
jolted US equities lower overnight – the Nasdaq falling 5.5% and S&P500 4.4%, both a multiple of the
prior session’s gains. That said, US term interest rates and FOMC rate expectations have also been
scaled back; so, while tech may come under greater scrutiny, a foundation for better outcomes across
the rest of the US economy may become evident in time. US data out this week continued to suggest
their economy is in robust health overall. And, if tensions in the Middle East abate, the FOMC’s

concerns over US inflation should subside. Gaining enough comfort for the FOMC to not hike in
December is a high bar, but the probability of further hikes in 2027 should continue to fall.
Turning then to the UK, the Bank of England raised Bank Rate by 25bps to 4.00% at their November
meeting as expected. On the Bank's baseline expectation, UK inflation is forecast to rise from 3.5%yr
at September to 5.00% by mid-2027. Activity data has also surprised to the upside in recent months,

though "there remains limited evidence of knock-on effects on other prices and wages".
In the press conference, Governor Bailey recognised the nascent improvement in energy price
pressures over the past 48 hours and the effectiveness of monetary policy but also conveyed that
"Unless progress towards resolving the conflict is made, or there is other news which softens the risk to
inflationary pressures, a further increase in Bank Rate may be warranted". Governor Bailey's tone and

comments made in the minutes by other Committee members did not suggest this was a decision that
needs to be made in December, but rather a risk to be judged as additional information is received.
This points to further tightening being more likely in early 2027 than before, and only if conditions / risks
warrant.

## Document 403: The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!

Source PDF: all report pdf/The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-11-18
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!
Date:    1 hour ago
The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live The AI Trade!

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
The AI Trade Is Dead, Long Live
The AI Trade!
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 17 Nov 2026
View in browser
Photo by Frolicsome Fairy / Unsplash

I. The AI Trade Is Dead!
"The King is dead, long live the King!" is a traditional proclamation
announcing a monarch's death while immediately saluting their successor
to show unbroken royal continuity. We think this proclamation applies to
the CEO of Nvidia and the broader AI trade.

### Page 2

Over the past few weeks, we have frequently discussed three key
constraints on the AI investment boom: physical, financial, and political.
Lately, all three have been in play. The AI buildout still needs more
semiconductors and more power. In recent months, the bond vigilantes
have driven up costs for AI borrowers, especially the neoclouds. Then
came the Democrats' midterm sweep, raising fears of growing political
opposition to data center construction.

Now we have to contend with the resignation of Nvidia's CEO Jensen
Huang. The alleged Huang honeytrap scandal adds another layer of
complexity to everyone's outlook for the AI industry.
On the financial front, under Huang, Nvidia has acted as the central bank
of the AI trade, holding $99 billion in equity investments across the AI
ecosystem as of July, plus $25 billion in commitments. It also announced
Wall Street partnerships to mobilize more than $500 billion in third-party

infrastructure financing. But Huang's influence extended beyond these
arrangements. His reputation as the Godfather of AI gave Nvidia-backed
companies a powerful vote of confidence, helping them attract capital from
other investors and lenders. His departure raises questions about both
sources of financial support.
On the political front, the Nvidia-Megaspeed relationship exposed three
potential export-control issues for the US: (1) Intermediaries have been

sending restricted chips to China. (2) China has been accessing overseas
computing capacity remotely. (3) The identities of end-users haven't been
properly vetted. The Commerce Department's latest restrictions aim to
address all three through tighter export controls, remote-access controls,
and due diligence requirements.
Physical constraints could also intensify if China retaliates by restricting
critical mineral exports, potentially disrupting semiconductor production

and the broader AI buildout.
Investors increasingly feared that these constraints could become
roadblocks, bringing the AI investment boom to an abrupt halt.
II. Long Live The AI Trade!

### Page 3

We anticipated buying opportunities in the stock market after the midterm
elections. We see more opportunities than we expected because of the
mounting panic over the AI trade. We view the constraints as speed
bumps that are slowing the AI race. Slowing the pace should reduce the
number of accidents. But the AI investment boom should continue
because the most important variable is demand for compute, which should
keep growing rapidly for the foreseeable future.

Nvidia's latest Q3 earnings and conference call today bolstered our
conviction. Here are our key takeaways:
(1) Another earnings beat. Q3 revenue of $115.8 billion exceeded
expectations of $109.0 billion, while adjusted EPS of $2.68 beat estimates
of $2.47. Data center revenue surged 110% y/y to $107.6 billion,
confirming robust compute demand.
(2) Stronger Q4 outlook. Management guided for Q4 revenue of $130
billion, above the consensus of $125 billion. Nvidia's next-generation Vera

Rubin platform has also begun shipping.
(3) Even stronger FY 2027 guidance. Interim CEO Collette Kress raised
projected revenue growth from 70% to 75%, easing concerns that the
scandal and tighter export restrictions would undermine Nvidia's outlook.
Notably, the forecast assumes no data center compute revenue from
China.
(4) Slowing financing activities. Kress reaffirmed Nvidia's support for
the AI ecosystem while emphasizing more selective neocloud financing.

This will undoubtedly be a speed bump for the neoclouds. We don't expect
a wave of bankruptcies in this space. Instead, slower compute expansion
should boost cloud usage pricing and revenues.
(5) No Jensen comeback. Nvidia's board confirmed that it does not
intend to reinstate Jensen Huang as CEO. Colette Kress remains interim
CEO as the search for a permanent successor continues.
Speed bumps reduce the risk of a boom-bust cycle. They are very useful

when an investment boom risks taking the next corner too fast. They
reduce crash risk.

### Page 4

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

## Document 434: What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What lessons can we learn to keep European industry competitive from the measures taken by Germany, as the cost of electricity continues to turn the screw_ (2026-10-22).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The bank
for a changing
world
WHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM
THE MEASURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO
TURN THE SCREW?
Marianne Mueller and Pascal Devaux
issue 19
21 October 2026
The 2022 energy crisis clearly highlighted the issue of electricity prices in Europe — and, more broadly, the issue of energy prices. As
a result, European manufacturers’ competitiveness has been severely undermined when compared with the United States and China.

By reigniting tensions over electricity prices, the conflict that began in the Middle East in late February 2026 further underscores the
continent’s vulnerability to fossil fuel imports, despite the expansion of renewable energy. Faced with an environment of potential sharp
and sudden price rises, European governments do not have the same room for manoeuvre.
While the additional costs borne by European manufacturers are mainly linked to the share of gas in national electricity mixes, we will

first illustrate that, in Germany, grid operation and maintenance costs, as well as taxation, are also contributing significantly to these
costs.
We will then explore the solutions proposed by the German government under the new European measures regulating state aid to
electricity-intensive industries.
Finally, our estimates will show that, while these measures make Germany more competitive against some European countries, they
do not place it on an equal competitive footing with the United States or China. Nevertheless, Germany has made significant progress

in decarbonising and making its electricity mix more flexible from a European perspective. This should help to ensure that electricity costs
are reduced in the medium term.

### Page 2

2
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoInsight 19 / 21 October 2026
The bank
for a changing
worldWHAT LESSONS CAN WE LEARN TO KEEP EUROPEAN INDUSTRY COMPETITIVE FROM THE MEA- SURES TAKEN BY GERMANY, AS THE COST OF ELECTRICITY CONTINUES TO TURN THE SCREW?
GAS: A KEY FACTOR IN THE ADDITIONAL ELECTRICITY COSTS
FACED BY EUROPEAN INDUSTRY
European manufacturers are still paying significantly higher elec -
tricity costs than their American and Chinese competitors. In 2019,

there was a 30% difference between the prices charged to European
electricity-intensive industries (corresponding to the lower end of the
industrial price range) and the average US industrial price. In 2025,
it stood at nearly 100% according to the IEA (2026) 1, after peaking at
an average of 148% between 2022 and 2023. There is currently a 50%
difference with their Chinese competitors2.
The disruption to Russian gas supplies since 2022 is behind the rising

gas prices, particularly as the EU has had to turn to liquefied natural
gas3 (LNG), which is more expensive than gas transported via pipe -
line and, above all, exposes Europe to global gas market conditions.
The growth of LNG has, in fact, intensified competition between the
major consumption regions, primarily Europe and Asia. European and
Asian prices now tend to move more in tandem (Chart 1). However, in
China, the prevalence of coal — a widely available local resource —

and a more regulated electricity price help to keep industrial prices
lower. At the same time, the United States enjoys plentiful domestic
gas, which is produced at a low cost, driven by the sharp rise in shale
oil and gas production over the past decade. By contrast, since 2022,
Europe has been more exposed to the cost of imported gas (90% of its
total consumption, according to Eurostat), while the United States and
China have enjoyed plentiful domestic resources.

Fluctuations and the level of gas prices are passed on to Eu -
ropean electricity prices via the ‘merit order’ marginal pricing
mechanism, with the wholesale price determined by the margi -
nal cost of the last (and most expensive) electricity source called
upon to meet demand.
As a result, gas plays a decisive role, with gas-fired power sta -
tions setting the price in around 40% of cases in Europe4, with si-
gnificant variations depending on national electricity mixes. It is

close to this average in Germany, below it in Spain (26%) and
France (17%), and above it in Italy (73%) 5 in 2025 6 7. Therefore,
electricity prices are still sensitive to fluctuations in gas prices,
even when electricity is not generated from gas (Chart 2). This
pass-through is particularly pronounced when demand rises
and requires the use of more expensive sources, or when supply
constraints arise (for example, during extreme heatwaves that
reduce wind-power generation or disrupt the cooling of nuclear

power plants).
1 Prices – Electricity 2026 – Analysis – IEA
2 The difference may be even larger for other manufacturers who do not receive the same support as electricity-intensive sectors.
3 Imports of Russian gas (via pipeline and LNG) accounted for around 45% of total European imports in 2021, compared with 14% in Q2 2026 (Bruegel, natural gas imports).
4 ACER, 2026, Key developments in EU electricity and gas markets, 2026 Monitoring report.
5 In Italy, in response to sharply rising gas prices in 2026, the government is currently negotiating a potential option of capping the prices of gas supplied to power stations in

order to limit the rise in the wholesale price of electricity. However, this can only be a temporary general mechanism, with the potentially major cost being borne by public funds
and/or households.
6 Eurelectric, 2026, Power Barometer.
7 EMBER, 2026, Latest energy shock reminds Europe of its risky reliance on gas.
8 Energy shock: 2026 vs. 2022 dashboard – 2nd October 2026 – Economic Studies – BNP Paribas
However, for the time being, the 2026 energy crisis is having a far

smaller effect on electricity prices than in 2022. On the one hand, the
2026 peak reached by the European TTF benchmark is still well below
that recorded in 20228.
In addition, there has been significant progress in decarbonising Eu -
ropean electricity mixes since 2022, which has helped to mitigate, on
average, the pass-through of gas prices to electricity prices.

SOME EUROPEAN COUNTRIES HAVE MANAGED TO STAY
COMPETITIVE, BUT GERMANY IS STILL THE EXCEPTION
Electricity prices partly vary due to countries’ electricity mixes
(Chart 3). Electricity-intensive manufacturers in countries where the
share of fossil energy is high face higher costs (Poland, Italy and Ire -
land) (Chart 4). Conversely, countries with a less carbon-intensive mix

(particularly France and Spain in recent times) are less directly ex -
posed to these fluctuations, with costs closer to the United States and
China (Chart 5).
The situation in Germany is paradoxical, as, despite progressively
greening its electricity mix and wholesale electricity prices (averaging
90 EUR/MWh over the 2023–2025 period) and average electricity costs
(across all industrial sectors) in line with the European average, the most
energy-intensive industries face higher costs than the European average

(and even more so compared with international competitors). This is
mainly due to grid costs and a tax burden above EU standards (Chart 6).
9 Eurostat data are used to compare prices with the rest of the EU. However, given that electricity prices are not uniform for businesses at a European level, there may be differences
between Eurostat and national sources. Jäger P., 2025, “Cautionary tale or model for Europe? How member states should react to Germany’s new electricity subsidy”, Hertie School,

Jacques Delors Centre.
10 P. Devaux, 2026, “European Union: low carbon transition and energy sovereignty, a path fraught with obstacles”
In the first half of 2026, for the most electricity-intensive industries,
the difference compared with the European average was 9%, based on
the prices excluding taxes and levies provided by Eurostat. This gap is
mainly due to the cost of accessing the grid. If all taxes and levies are
included, the difference rises to 21% (Chart 7)9.

EUROPE’S APPROACH OF ADOPTING BOTH STRUCTURAL
PROGRAMMES AND TARGETED SUPPORT MEASURES
European programmes to support the low-carbon transition pursue
objectives relating to climate action (reducing greenhouse gas emis -
sions), sovereignty10 (increasing energy self-sufficiency) and competi -
tiveness (reducing the volatility and costs associated with fossil fuels).
The RePowerEU programme, which was launched in 2022 in response
to the energy crisis, primarily aims to reduce imports of Russian gas

and, more generally, to accelerate the low-carbon transition. Although
Europe has reduced its dependence on imports of Russian fossil fuels,
there has still not been enough progress around the energy transition
and cross-border connectivity. This is due to governance constraints

and struggles in implementing the programme in practice within each
Member State11.
State aid is still the main vehicle for this. In response to the 2021–
2022 energy crisis, the European Union temporarily relaxed the rules
on this type of aid in order to support businesses facing rising energy
prices. Any intervention of this kind, provided that it is selective and

could affect trade within the single market, must be authorised in ad -
vance by the European Commission in principle. Since June 2025, the
Clean Industrial Deal State Aid Framework (CISAF) has provided a more
sustainable framework for these kinds of interventions. In particular,
this framework, which runs until the end of 2030, authorises Member
States to grant temporary aid to electricity-intensive businesses to off-
set the cost of electricity. The aid must be targeted and come with

decarbonisation commitments12. Against the backdrop of the crisis in
the Middle East, the temporary METSAF scheme, adopted in April 2026,
helped (in relation to measures falling under the CISAF) to increase the
proportion of additional costs offset by governments from 50% to 70%,
thereby offering additional support to the most at-risk sectors.
GERMANY IS STILL SUFFERING FROM INSUFFICIENT IN-
VESTMENT IN ITS ELECTRICITY NETWORKS, WHICH THE
AID SCHEMES ARE ATTEMPTING TO OFFSET

The issue of electricity costs is a particularly hot issue in Germany,
given the importance of industry to the economy (around 20% of va -
lue added, one of the highest levels in Europe) and its exposure to
international competition. Furthermore, the delay in modernising
electricity infrastructure (only 22.6% of the high-voltage lines planned
for 2025 had actually been completed by that date) is causing bottle -
necks between the production areas in the north and the consump -

11 European Court of Auditors, 2026, “Implementation of the REPowerEU plan must be stepped up”.
12 It applies to the most electricity-intensive activities, which are seeing their competitiveness threatened by international competition, and follows the 4x50 principle: 50% of
electricity consumption is eligible for a subsidy; Of the eligible proportion, the subsidy may not exceed 50% of the wholesale price;
The final wholesale price paid by the company must not be less than €50/MWh;

50% of the aid received must be reinvested in decarbonisation (renewable energy generation, storage and improvements in energy efficiency); Around 90 sectors are covered by this
reform, accounting for approximately 2,000 companies, including in the chemical, steel and paper industries. The scheme, which is strictly regulated, will run for just three years
(from 1st January 2026 to the end of 2028) at an estimated total cost of between EUR 3 and 5 bn, financed by the special Climate and Transformation Fund.

13 Bundesnetzagentur Monitoring Report (2015) and Bundesnetzagentur Monitoring Report (2025). These costs mainly cover the costs of physical infrastructure; operational,
regulatory and compliance costs; and costs associated with using the transmission and distribution network.
14 The Industriestrompreis (industrial electricity price) is the public subsidy mechanism aiming to reduce the price of electricity for some industries.
15 The indirect carbon cost compensation mechanism is a government support measure for electricity-intensive industries, which aims to offset the cost of carbon-emission

allowances for energy producers.
tion centres in the south. As a result, the costs of accessing services
have increased fivefold over 10 years (rising from EUR 1 bn in 2014
to EUR 5.2 bn in 2024 13). At the same time, as we have already seen,
taxation and grid charges are hitting industry hard, with levies on large
consumers far exceeding the European average despite some support
mechanisms being put in place for the most electricity-intensive com-

panies (such as the Bandlastprivileg measure to reduce grid tariffs).
As a result, German industrial production has suffered in particular
due to rising energy prices in recent years, including electricity, with
electricity-intensive industries being especially affected (Chart 8).
In order to cope with these higher electricity costs than the European
average, Germany has been using European support schemes to reduce
the cost of electricity for businesses, particularly since the Merz go -

vernment took office in May 2025. The main scheme — the Industries-
trompreis14, made possible by the CISAF — has been approved by the
European Commission for the 2026–2028 period. While it is less bene-
ficial than the indirect carbon cost (ICC) offsetting included in electri -
city prices15, it has a broader scope, meaning that businesses ineligible
for the ICC can benefit from it. Other measures complement this targe-
ted support by addressing other components of the electricity bill. As a

result, the electricity tax has been set at the European minimum for
manufacturing sectors, while the gas-storage tax has been abolished.
The government is also subsidising grid fees, which should halve them.
Overall, according to our estimates based on 2024 prices, the final
price of electricity for these industries could fall by around a quarter
(to 116 EUR/MWh for industries with annual consumption of between
70 and 150 GWh, compared with a European average of 123 EUR/MWh)

(Chart 9). Germany’s advantage over Central and Eastern European
countries, where electricity costs are generally already higher, looks
set to increase.

However, even when taking all of the subsidies into account, the price
of electricity in Germany would remain higher than the levels seen in
France, Spain or Portugal (and even more so in the United States and
China).
FISCAL MEASURES: LIMITED ROOM FOR MANOEUVRE
There are a number of limitations to these European schemes. For
the country implementing them, lower electricity costs mean that

its industry becomes more competitive against industries from other
EU countries, but this does very little to address the disadvantage that
it faces compared with the United States and China. Therefore, this
measure will unlikely be enough to counteract the loss of market share
seen vis-à-vis the United States and China. In addition, the support
schemes are restrictive (conditional on investment in the low-carbon
transition) and are transitional. Finally, their cost is partly passed on

to the public purse or to consumers who do not receive this support
(in Germany, the cost of the new energy price support measures is
estimated at EUR 14.3 bn in 2026, or 0.3% of GDP16).
Some European manufacturers have proposed abolishing or reducing
the carbon tax as a solution to getting electricity prices down quickly.
In Germany, this tax accounted for 14% of the electricity price in the
first half of 2025, which is higher than the EU average (11%). However,

this proposal is already covered by the indirect carbon cost offsetting
mechanism for electricity-intensive industries exposed to carbon al -
lowance prices. In addition, while adjustments are being made — no -
tably to slow the rate at which allowances are reduced — it seems
highly unlikely that the scheme will be significantly challenged. It is
one of the cornerstones of Europe’s policy to combat climate change,
which has been effective thus far, as shown by the falling greenhouse

gas emissions from industry. More broadly, the gradual introduction of
a carbon border adjustment mechanism (CBAM) should mitigate com-
petition distortions caused by environmental practices17.
IN GERMANY AND THE REST OF EUROPE, HEAVY INVEST -
MENT IS DRIVING UP ELECTRICITY BILLS IN THE SHORT
TERM
In response to the challenge of the low-carbon transition and indus -
trial competitiveness, European governments must strike a balance
between decarbonising the electricity mix and ensuring a supply of

energy that is flexible enough (particularly from controllable sources)
to guarantee stable electricity systems. The integration of an increa -
sing proportion of intermittent renewable energy sources requires
massive investment in transmission and storage infrastructure (statio-
nary batteries). This expenditure, which places a long-term burden on
grid costs, reduces the scope for support schemes (particularly public
ones) to address competitiveness gaps. In Germany, the cost of the

transition is estimated at between EUR 122 bn, according to the go -
vernment (2.8% of GDP), and EUR 200 bn, according to the DIHK18 (4.6%
of GDP), by 2030–2035. If we take the ECB’s assumption19 that govern-
ments will cover around 17% of these investments, the annual public
16 The Industriestrompreis is estimated at around EUR 1.3 bn per year, the subsidy for network charges is projected to amount to EUR 6.5 bn in 2026, the abolition of the gas sur -

charge around EUR 3.4 bn, and the reduction in the electricity tax nearly EUR 3 bn per year. Furthermore, the ICC had cost EUR 2.3 bn in 2024 for costs incurred in 2023.

expenditure burden of the transition in Germany would be between
0.5 and 0.8 percentage points of GDP by 2030. However, this budgetary
pressure comes at the same time as three other budgetary increases:
rising social expenditure linked to an ageing population; the necessary
increase to defence budgets to meet NATO’s target of 3.5% of GDP by
2029; and the rising cost of debt-servicing20.
Germany has already made significant progress in maximising its use

of renewable energy, putting it at the head of the pack in Europe. The
country generates 20% of Europe’s renewable electricity (solar and
wind) and accounts for 30% of installed storage capacity (BESS) 21, far
ahead of other European countries (ranking third globally). However,
despite this momentum, Germany’s dependence on gas has not yet
decreased. As a matter of fact, it is only partially offsetting the planned
closure of coal-fired power stations and the phasing out of nuclear

reactors. Furthermore, the intermittent nature of solar and wind power
could prolong this dependence in the short and medium term. In order
to ensure that the German electricity system is flexible, new invest -
ments in gas-fired power plants, supported by public subsidies, are
planned22.

SUBSIDIES ARE HELPING TO ADDRESS THE EMERGENCY
CAUSED BY ENERGY CRISES WITHOUT RESTORING THE
INTERNATIONAL COMPETITIVENESS OF ENERGY-INTEN-
SIVE INDUSTRIES
In response to the impact of energy crises on industrial competitive -
ness, European governments are finding themselves having to strike a
delicate balance between providing short-term support to industries

exposed to increased international competition and continuing — if
not, accelerating — energy transition policies which will result in hi -
gher costs for electricity-network users for several years to come.
Beyond direct economic support measures, which are difficult to sus -
tain long term, four priority areas are emerging:
1/ Demonstrating flexibility in implementing European directives in
order to factor in national specificities and the differing timeframes

between energy crises (which can lead to irreversible industrial conse-
quences) and reforms (which have a longer-term horizon). The relaxa-
tion of the European carbon tax mechanism 23 appears to be a step
towards this.
2/ Continuing efforts to decarbonise the electricity mix, which are still
the main vehicle for sustainably reducing electricity prices for Euro -
pean industries in the medium and long term.
3/ Optimising the use of electricity networks on the demand side, in

particular through smart grids, a flagship element of the Commission’s
COM (2026) 600 reform presented last July24.
4/ Coordinating European electricity networks in order to harness the
complementarities between national systems.
Article completed on 19 October 2026

在進行中，須總統立法才能恢復，可以是談判交換
條件的一部分，醫療補助的刪減是已立法，分階段
生效，無法被推翻，只能延後刪減，其中對工作的
要求將於年底生效早於新國會就任，12/11臨時撥
款法案談判是可以納入談判作為局部修正的時間點。
若醫療補助刪減延後，將有利於醫院，若ACA補貼
部分恢復將有利於ACA型管理式醫療業者的投保人

數止跌回升。兩黨均支持對藥物管理福利機構(PBM)
的監管，已有法案讓Part D藥價與PBM的報酬脫鉤，
回扣必須全數轉交給計畫方，2028年生效，現業者
多將Part D回扣與價差收入轉為固定費用應對，對
激進者業務分拆的主張則可能以聽證、調查形式出
現而非馬上立法。但在州層級，阿肯色州禁止PBM
擁有藥局的法律在州政府上訴中，CVS已決定1H28
分拆藥局成兩家獨立公司應對。就財報部分，UNH
3Q26財報仍維持業者利潤優先於規模與核保紀律，
合約重定價與刪除不賺錢合約，運用AI改善營運效
率、提升服務品質，在藥物福利管理主動讓利降低
政策風險。聯合醫療保險(UHC)因保費重定價與醫
療成本管控抵銷投保人數萎縮，Optum Health退
出虧損市場，聚焦高回報價值導向醫療運營紀律，
使此兩業務部門利潤率提升優於預期。商業保險醫
療成本趨勢依然高，但已趨於穩定，醫療優勢計畫
(MA)利潤率已接近2%-4%目標區間上限，公司自
評2027/2028年接近75%會員可望維持在4星以上
方案。醫療補助仍虧損，將透過州費率調整將緩步

走出低谷。4Q26醫療成本趨勢雖因季節性因素走升，
為今年獲利低點，但明年多項利潤率改善可期，維
持兩位數獲利成長。
3
資料來源：UNH、彭博，2026/10/16，中國信託商業銀行整理，2026/9/30

事件  1
11/17 輝達第三季營收 1,158 億美元、年增 103% ，第四季指引 1,300 億美元
五⼤重點
1   輝達 11/17 公布第三季（截⾄ 10/25 ）營收 1,158 億美元，季增 20% 、年增 103% ，⾼於市場預期
約 1,088 億美元；資料中⼼營收 1,076 億美元，⾮ GAAP （⾮⼀般公認會計原則）每股盈餘 2.68 美

元。
2   第四季營收指引 1,300 億美元（正負 3% ，含多⼀週），⽑利率指引由第三季 74.9% 降⾄ 71% ⾄
72% ；公司指引未計⼊任何中國資料中⼼運算營收，第三季對中國 Hopper 出貨為 24 億美元。

### Page 2

3   財務⻑說明，第三季⽑利率⼩降反映 Vera Rubin 初期放量；存貨由 316 億升⾄ 397 億美元、應收
帳款天數由 60 天升⾄ 64 天，公司歸因於 Rubin 出貨節奏與部分投資級⼤客戶延⻑付款條件。
4   輝達正常盤收跌 0.44% ⾄ 155.46 美元，盤後漲約 3.8% ⾄ 161.35 美元；暫代執⾏⻑克雷斯稱
Rubin 「爆發性起步」，董事會主席表⽰無意讓⿈仁勳回任，將考慮內外部⼈選。
5   供應與產能承諾由 2,790 億增⾄ 3,900 億美元，主要為多年期記憶體採購，未來承諾總額 5,180
億美元；同⽇放空機構渾⽔（ Muddy Waters ）宣布放空輝達，治理疑慮未除。
三⼤結論
1   財報確認既有 AI 需求仍在加速交付，營收超預期與 Rubin 量產回應了換帥後對產品節奏的疑
慮；晶圓代⼯、先進封裝與記憶體的訂單能⾒度獲⽀撐，但⽑利指引下滑代表新平台爬坡成本由
輝達先吸收。
2   存貨、應收天數與採購承諾同步上升，且部分⼤客戶付款條件延⻑，顯⽰營收擴張伴隨營運資

⾦占⽤增加；這與⾼槓桿新雲端依賴外部融資的⾵險相連，供應鏈評價仍需以回款與現⾦流驗
證。
3   當輝達後續季度應收天數回落、存貨隨 Rubin 出貨下降，且台積電營收維持指引時，應分批維
持晶圓代⼯、先進封裝與記憶體核⼼部位；若新管理層縮減對新雲端的擔保或回款再延⻑，應先
降低 ODM 與⾼波動雲端衛星。
事件  2
11/17 紐約 Fed 總裁威廉斯稱通膨頑固，仍需升息但無須⼤動作
五⼤重點
1   紐約 Fed 總裁威廉斯 11/17 在紐約 Axios 論壇表⽰，通膨「頑固偏⾼」，關稅與能源衝擊正擴散⾄
更廣泛的通膨數據， Fed 要讓通膨回到 2% 仍有更多⼯作要做。
2   他認為經濟強勁且持續轉強、勞動市場穩健，「需求明顯跑贏供給」，但當前情勢不需要採取激
進的政策⾏動；⽬前聯邦資⾦利率⽬標區間為 3.75% ⾄ 4.00% 。
3   威廉斯為 FOMC （聯邦公開市場委員會）常任票委，談話後利率期貨隱含 12 ⽉升息⼀碼機率約
96% 、升息兩碼約 4% ，⼤幅升息的可能幾乎被排除。

4   他同時指出 AI 產業融資「變得更不透明且複雜」，但⽬前未看到 AI 帶來⾦融穩定⾵險；美國 10
年期公債殖利率 11/17 收約 5.10% ，仍處 5% 上⽅。

### Page 3

5   同⽇聖路易 Fed 總裁穆薩萊姆表⽰，實際政策利率需要再上升約 1 個百分點，貨幣政策必須⽴即
反應，鷹派官員主張的緊縮幅度明顯⼤於威廉斯。
三⼤結論
1   常任票委確認 12 ⽉升息⼀碼是基準、但排除兩碼，政策討論由「是否升息」轉為「還要升幾
次」；短端利率將停留⾼檔，⻑天期成⻑股的折現率壓⼒延續。
2   官員對緊縮幅度分歧，核⼼服務與能源擴散的數據將決定終點利率；短天期國庫券兼具收益與
再配置彈性，⻑天期公債在通膨與供給壓⼒未改善前仍應有限度配置。
3   當 11/19 公布的會議紀要與後續核⼼通膨顯⽰多數委員⽀持連續升息時，應維持短券核⼼並延
後增加⻑債久期；若核⼼服務⽉增回落且公債標售需求改善，才逐步延⻑存續期間。
事件  3
11/17 美國 10 ⽉零售銷售⽉增 0.2% ，⼯業⽣產⽉減 0.4%
五⼤重點
1   美國商務部普查局公布 10 ⽉零售銷售初值 7,482 億美元，⽉增 0.2% ，符合預期、低於 9 ⽉修正
後的 0.8% ；排除汽⾞及零件⽉增 0.6% ，整體年增 6.8% 。

2  Fed 公布 10 ⽉⼯業⽣產⽉減 0.4% ，遜於預期的⽉增 0.1% ，主因公⽤事業產出⽉減 4.3% 、礦業
減 0.8% ；製造業產出反⽽⽉增 0.3% ，產能利⽤率由 76.8% 降⾄ 76.4% 。
3   普查局說明，零售⽉增 0.2% 的信賴區間為正負 0.4% ，統計上無法確認為正成⻑；排除汽⾞後
⽉增 0.6% ⾼於整體，代表汽⾞及零件銷售下滑（推算）。
4   勞⼯統計局同⽇公布 10 ⽉進⼝物價⽉增 1.1% ，⾼於預期的 0.7% ，進⼝成本持續上升，與威廉
斯所說關稅與能源衝擊正向更廣泛物價擴散的判斷⼀致。
5   零售年增 6.8% 含物價上漲因素；商務部先前公布 9 ⽉個⼈儲蓄率已降⾄ 3.8% ，家庭以降低儲蓄
維持⽀出，⾯對油價或就業衝擊的緩衝變薄。
三⼤結論
1  10 ⽉消費放緩但未轉弱，⼯業⽣產下滑主要來⾃公⽤事業⽽⾮製造業，整體仍符合景氣擴
張；需求韌性加上進⼝物價⾛⾼，⽀持 Fed 在 12 ⽉續升息的基準。
2   名⽬零售成⻑含價格因素、家庭儲蓄緩衝下降，消費類股應區分客群收⼊穩定的企業與依賴信

⽤擴張的商品零售，不宜以名⽬銷售成⻑直接推估利潤。

### Page 4

3   當 11 ⽉零售、實質消費與初領失業⾦⼈數同時轉弱時，應提⾼現⾦與⾼品質債券⽐重；若消
費維持⽽核⼼通膨仍⾼，股票與⻑債可能同步承壓，應維持短券與現⾦流較強的股票。
事件  4
11/17 美軍空襲伊朗格什姆島⾶彈基地，布蘭特原油收 93.14 美元
五⼤重點
1   美國軍⽅官員表⽰，美軍戰機 11/17 以精準彈藥空襲伊朗⾰命衛隊位於荷莫茲海峽格什姆島的
⾶彈發射場，稱是回應伊朗針對美⽅⼈員與設施的攻擊，屬⾃衛⾏動。
2   伊朗地⽅官員稱攻擊未造成住宅與基礎設施損害；⾰命衛隊表⽰以無⼈機攻擊巴林伊薩空軍基
地的美軍雷達，海軍並稱第四艘油輪在荷莫茲海峽東部觸雷。
3   布蘭特原油 11/17 收 93.14 美元、漲 0.76% ，⻄德州原油收 86.01 美元、漲 0.34% ；盤中卡達外交
部稱美伊調解仍在持續，油價⼀度轉跌，漲幅受限。
4   伊朗總統裴澤斯基安表⽰，伊朗正以外交途徑尋求結束「不戰不和」局⾯，外⻑並已向阿曼蘇
丹通報結束戰爭的倡議，軍事衝突與和談訊號同時存在。

5   美軍先前稱已清除荷莫茲主要航道⽔雷，第四艘油輪觸雷顯⽰航道⾵險未消除；該海峽平時承
載全球約五分之⼀⽯油運量，美國仍對伊朗港⼝實施海上封鎖。
三⼤結論
1   美⽅由 11/16 暫緩打擊轉為 11/17 有限度空襲，地緣溢價重回油價，但調解仍在進⾏、布蘭特僅
⼩漲，市場仍把衝突定價為有限升級⽽⾮全⾯斷供。
2   油價停在 90 美元附近，能源通膨向核⼼物價擴散的⾵險仍在，與 Fed 官員對能源衝擊的擔憂⼀
致；原油與能源股宜維持⼩型事件部位，不以單⼀軍事新聞擴⼤配置。
3   當荷莫茲觸雷與攻擊擴⼤、布蘭特重回 100 美元以上時，應提⾼能源衛星作為通膨避險；若美
伊和談取得進展且供給修復，應降低原油部位並留意上游獲利下修。
事件  5
11/17 台股再跌 947 點收 40,092 點，外資連 2 ⽇賣超
五⼤重點

### Page 5

1   台灣加權指數 11/17 收 40,092.11 點，下跌 947.97 點、跌幅 2.31% ，盤中最低 39,786.45 點；櫃買
指數跌 3.92% ，上市櫃下跌 1,767 家、上漲僅 454 家。
2   台積電收 2,170 元、跌 2.03% ；上市三⼤法⼈合計賣超 547.2 億元，其中外資賣超 380.9 億元、
⾃營商賣超 157.0 億元，外資近 20 ⽇累計賣超約 1.14 兆元。
3   ⾼盛 11/17 將台股評等由「加碼」降⾄「中⽴」，加權指數⽬標由 57,500 點降⾄ 50,000 點；台股
早盤⼀度跌 3.1% 後短暫翻紅，終場仍收低。
4   兩⽇累計下跌約 3,583 點、跌幅約 8.2% （推算）；賣壓延續輝達換帥後的治理疑慮與美國遠端
算⼒管制，美股晶⽚股在輝達財報前同樣遭到賣壓。
5   上市融資餘額近 20 ⽇減少約 83 萬張，仍在近⼀年中段；台股市值加權本益⽐約 18.9 倍、預估本
益⽐ 17.5 倍，評價已隨指數回落，但外資賣壓尚未停歇。

三⼤結論
1   兩⽇重挫主要反映 AI ⿓頭治理與出⼝管制帶來的評價修正；輝達隨後公布的營收與 Rubin 出貨
優於預期，台灣上游交付基本⾯未⾒轉弱，股價與基本⾯的落差擴⼤。
2   外資持續賣超、企業保留美元與美台利差擴⼤，台幣承壓格局未變；台股⾼評分與台幣低評分
可同時成⽴，持有台股的本幣報酬須另計匯率影響。
3   當 11/20 公布的 10 ⽉外銷訂單延續強勁、外資賣超收斂時，應分批維持晶圓代⼯與先進封裝核
⼼部位；若融資再擴張或客戶回款轉弱，應先降低 ODM 與⾼波動中游衛星。
事件  6
11/17 Anthropic 獲准在那斯達克掛牌，維持每股 70 ⾄ 78 美元發⾏區間
五⼤重點
1  Anthropic 11/17 向美國證管會提交修正版 S-1 ，發⾏規模與條件不變，每股價格區間維持 70 ⾄
78 美元，並獲准以代號 ANT 在那斯達克全球精選市場掛牌，預計 11/19 開始交易。
2   本次發⾏ 8.0135 億股新股，創辦⼈另出售 2,365 萬股；以區間中值 74 美元計，新股募資約 593

億美元（推算），另有 260 億美元⾮投票權 N 類股私募同步進⾏。
3   發⾏完成後亞⾺遜將持有 14.7% 流通股本、 33.9% N 類股，若其可轉債全數轉股將升⾄
23.8% 、 48.2% ；亞⾺遜與 Google 兩⼤客戶合計持股逾 28% 。

### Page 6

4   招股⽂件顯⽰， 2026 年 1 ⾄ 9 ⽉營收 332.4 億美元，第三季 169.6 億美元， 10 ⽉底年化營收約 810
億美元；同期營業虧損 12.6 億美元，帳⾯淨損多數為⾮現⾦評價調整。
5   以價格區間計，隱含市值約 9,175 億⾄ 1.02 兆美元； Altimeter 執⾏⻑表⽰已申請 50 億美元配售
額度，富時羅素通知最快可於 12/11 納⼊羅素 1000 指數。
三⼤結論
1  AI 模型業者⾸度以公開市場定價，⽤量成⻑能否抵銷 Token （計費單位）降價，將直接影響市
場對 AI 終端變現的信⼼，並回頭牽動上游算⼒需求的評價。
2   年化營收不等於已實現收⼊，帳⾯虧損也含⼤量⾮現⾦項⽬，評價須拆解使⽤量、單位收費、
算⼒成本與股權稀釋；⼤客戶交叉持股使營收與雲端資本⽀出⾼度連動。
3   當掛牌後付費留存與⽑利改善、算⼒⽀出占營收⽐續降時，應以正向看待 AI 軟體與上游算⼒需
求；若 Token 價格跌幅快於⽤量成⻑或上市後估值劇烈修正，應降低對模型與軟體⾼估值部位的

容忍度。
事件  7
11/16 Fed 主席華許提出 AI 三種經濟情境，納⼊脆弱性評估
五⼤重點
1  Fed 主席華許 11/16 為舊⾦⼭ Fed 活動準備的講稿表⽰， Fed 幕僚已開始推演 AI 加速普及對美國
經濟的不同情境，並強調這些情境不是預測。
2   第⼀種情境是 AI 推升⽣產⼒與成⻑、由雇主與勞⼯共享繁榮，第⼆種是經濟活動增加的好處被
⼤規模失業抵銷；第三種是技術不如預期，引發股價下跌與經濟放緩。
3   華許稱 Fed 的⾓⾊不是⽀持或反對 AI ，⽽是評估 AI 可能帶來的潛在脆弱性，包括遭不良⾏為者
利⽤造成擾亂，以及對勞動市場的重⼤衝擊。
4  Fed 主席把「 AI 不如預期導致股市下跌」列為正式情境，代表 AI 投資回落對⾦融市場與景氣的
衝擊，已被納⼊央⾏的政策評估範圍。
5   講稿未對 12 ⽉利率決策表態； Fed 10 ⽉以 10 ⽐ 2 維持利率 3.75% ⾄ 4.00% ，⽬前利率期貨已幾
乎完全定價 12 ⽉升息⼀碼，短期政策仍由通膨數據主導。

三⼤結論

### Page 7

1  Fed 把 AI 泡沫破裂列為情境，意味 AI 資本⽀出若未能變現，貨幣政策可能同時⾯對股價修正與
景氣放緩； AI 相關資產的評價不能只看需求成⻑，也要看現⾦回收。
2   ⼤規模失業情境將改變 Fed 對就業⽬標的判斷，⽣產⼒情境則可能降低通膨壓⼒；這是⻑期框
架，短期利率仍以核⼼通膨與就業數據為依據。
3   當 AI 企業⾃由現⾦流持續為負、融資成本上升且變現覆蓋度下滑時，應降低⾼槓桿雲端與⾼估
值 AI 衛星；若雲端營收持續加速並帶動現⾦流改善，才提⾼ AI 供應鏈的⾵險權重。
事件  8
11/17 歐盟執委會秋季預測：法國今年⾚字 5.5% ，明年成⻑下修⾄ 0.6%
五⼤重點
1   歐盟執委會 11/17 公布秋季經濟預測，歐元區 2026 與 2027 年 GDP 均成⻑ 1.2% 、 2028 年 1.3% ；
2026 年通膨預估 3.2% ， 2027 年 3.1% ， 2028 年才回落⾄ 2.1% 。
2   法國 2026 年財政⾚字預估擴⼤⾄ GDP 的 5.5% ，⾼於前次預測 5.1% 與政府⽬標 5.4% ； 2027 年

僅⼩降⾄ 5.4% ，仍遠⾼於 5% ⽬標， 2027 年政府債務由 120.2% 上修⾄ 122.1% 。
3   執委會說明，法國經濟成⻑低迷與公債利息⽀出增加，將抵銷 2027 年預算中的⼤規模財政整
頓；法國 2027 年成⻑預測由 1.1% ⼤幅下修⾄ 0.6% 。
4   德國 2027 年成⻑預測由 0.9% 上修⾄ 1.0% 、⻄班⽛由 1.9% 升⾄ 2.1% 、義⼤利由 0.6% 升⾄
0.7% ；歐元區整體⾚字將由 2025 年 3.0% 升⾄ 2026 年 3.5% 。
5   法國 CAC 40 指數 11/17 收跌 1.62% ⾄ 8,216.78 點；同⽇法國爆發新⼀輪⽰威，內政部統計全國
約 14.6 萬⼈參與，政治與財政壓⼒仍未緩解。
三⼤結論
1   歐元區通膨預估到 2027 年仍⾼於 3% ，⽀持 ECB 維持緩步升息路徑；成⻑僅約 1.2% ，政策緊縮
與能源成本將持續壓抑企業獲利，景氣擴張但⼒道有限。
2   法國⾚字與債務上修、成⻑下修，是區域中最脆弱的⼀環；德國與南歐預測反⽽上修，主權⾵

險未全⾯擴散，不宜把法國壓⼒外推為全歐危機。
3   當法國預算審議再陷僵局、法德利差擴⼤時，應降低法國內需與主權債曝險；歐洲股市維持中
性偏審慎，偏好低負債、具海外收⼊與成本轉嫁能⼒的公司，待 PMI 廣度與能源改善再增加週期
曝險。

### Page 8

事件  9
11/17 美銀經理⼈調查：科技股淨超配⽐例降⾄ 12% ，創 2006 年來最⼤降幅
五⼤重點
1   美國銀⾏ 11/17 公布 11 ⽉全球基⾦經理⼈調查（ 11/6 ⾄ 11/12 進⾏），科技股淨超配⽐例由 10 ⽉
的 37% 降⾄ 12% ，為 2006 年 6 ⽉以來最⼤降幅。
2   創紀錄的淨 38% 受訪者認為企業過度投資 AI ， 52% 預期未來六個⽉將有⼤型雲端業者宣布削減
資本⽀出；淨 32% 預期全球 CPI （消費者物價指數）未來 12 個⽉下降。
3   美銀策略師哈特尼特表⽰，市場對 AI 繁榮的狂熱已經落幕，投資⼈強烈呼籲企業暫緩 AI 相關投
⼊。
4   調查期間涵蓋 11/12 輝達換帥前後；經理⼈⼤舉減碼科技，與輝達 11/17 公布營收年增 103% 、
Rubin 量產的基本⾯形成明顯落差。
5   費城半導體指數 11/17 盤中⼀度跌逾 3% ，終場收漲 0.34% ⾄ 9,642.88 點，仍較 52 週⾼點 14,655

點低約 34% （推算），科技股已⼤幅修正。
三⼤結論
1   經理⼈⼤幅減碼科技反映對 AI 資本⽀出回報的疑慮，⽽⾮已確認的需求下滑；上游財報與出貨
仍強，情緒與基本⾯的落差可能放⼤科技股的雙向波動。
2   若⼤型雲端業者真的削減資本⽀出，衝擊將先落在依賴外部融資的新雲端與中游組裝；具定價
權與現⾦流的晶圓代⼯、記憶體⿓頭受影響相對較⼩。
3   當雲端業者下⼀季財報確認資本⽀出維持、且科技持股已降⾄低檔時，可分批增加⾼品質上
游；若有⼤型雲端業者正式下修資本⽀出，應先降低 ODM 、⾼槓桿雲端與⾼估值軟體部位。
事件  10
11/17 京東第三季營收年增 3.5% 、淨利 65 億元，美股 ADR 收跌 7.66%
五⼤重點
1   京東 11/17 在港交所公告第三季營收 3,096 億元⼈⺠幣，年增 3.5% ；歸屬普通股股東淨利 65 億
元，⾼於去年同期 53 億元，淨利率由 1.8% 升⾄ 2.1% 。
2   ⾮美國公認會計準則淨利 83 億元，去年同期 58 億元，⾮ GAAP 淨利率由 1.9% 升⾄ 2.7% ，獲利

成⻑明顯快於營收。

### Page 9

3   京東港股 11/17 收跌 2.68% ⾄ 105.2 港元，美股 ADR 收跌 7.66% ⾄ 25.43 美元，接近 52 週低點
24.51 美元。
4   營收較去年同期僅增加約 105 億元⼈⺠幣（推算）；國家統計局 11/16 公布 10 ⽉社會消費品零售
年增僅 0.4% ，與平台營收低速成⻑相互印證。
5   淨利率雖升⾄ 2.1% ，仍屬低檔，獲利改善主要來⾃利潤率提升⽽⾮銷售規模擴張。
三⼤結論
1   京東獲利率提升但營收僅低個位數成⻑，反映中國居⺠消費與內需尚未修復；平台股獲利改善
不等於消費需求回溫，股價反應偏弱顯⽰市場更重視營收動能。
2   中國 10 ⽉零售與固定投資轉弱，政策尚未修復居⺠收⼊與資產價格；中國股市宜維持選擇性
衛星，優先財務健全的出⼝技術製造，內需平台不宜全⾯提⾼權重。
3   當中國零售、⺠間投資與居⺠收⼊連續改善、平台營收重回雙位數成⻑時，才考慮提⾼中國內
需股權重；若零售持續低於 1% 且價格競爭延續，應維持低配並以已知⽀出匹配⼈⺠幣部位。

待確認
1. 推算值：事件 3 「汽⾞及零件銷售下滑」由整體與排除汽⾞增幅推算；事件 5 兩⽇累計跌點與跌幅；事件 6 新股募資
約 593 億美元；事件 9 費半距 52 週⾼點跌幅；事件 10 營收增加額。
2. 單⼀來源或⼆⼿來源：事件 1 市場預期 1,088 億美元、盤後漲幅為媒體與報價資料；事件 2 利率期貨機率與 10 年期殖
利率 5.10% 為市場報價，威廉斯為論壇談話⾮官⽅講稿；事件 6 營收、虧損、隱含市值與私募⾦額取⾃招股⽂件的第
三⽅整理，尚未直接核對 SEC 原⽂；事件 7 華許講稿內容取⾃快訊，未取得 Fed 官網全⽂；事件 9 為美銀調查轉述。
3. 事件 6 Anthropic 預計 11/18 定價、 11/19 交易，最終價格與規模尚未確定；事件 1 管理層在法說會提及的年度成⻑⽬
標未取得逐字稿，未寫⼊。
4. 尚未公布：英國 10 ⽉ CPI （ 11/18 ）、 10 ⽉ FOMC 會議紀要（ 11/19 ）、台灣 10 ⽉外銷訂單（ 11/20 ）、川普訪華與

11/19 深圳三⽅會晤結果、德國內閣對 ECB ⼈事的決定。
5. 與上⼀輪⽐對（「全球⼗⼤財經事件｜ 2026/11/17 07:51 （台灣時間）」）：已列之台股 11/16 重挫、美國商務部遠端
算⼒管制、美股 11/16 科技股重挫、哈瑪克談話、紐約州製造業指數、布蘭特 11/16 下跌、中國 10 ⽉社零與⼯業增加
值、⽇本第三季 GDP 、中國證實三⽅會晤、英國 2 年期殖利率觸及 5% ，本輪均未再列為事件；事件 10 僅以⼀句背景
提及中國 10 ⽉社零。
6. 新事實晚於上⼀輪資料同步時間（ 11/17 07:51 ）：事件 4 美軍空襲格什姆島（台灣時間 11/17 23:55 起）、事件 5 台股
11/17 收盤（台灣時間 11/17 13:30 ），均以新事實為主體；事件 1 輝達財報與上⼀輪的股價事件屬不同公告。

財政⼤⾂城內實的原則性談話，份量不⾜；歐洲、⽇本公司無⾜夠份量的財報或公告；中美國際政治經濟競爭⽅⾯，

## Document 580: 凱基投顧_Takeaway_6533 晶心科_許芝瑄_20261113

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_Takeaway_6533 晶心科_許芝瑄_20261113.pdf

### Page 1

十一月 13, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R   1

法說重點摘要

晶心科 
(6533 TT, NT$249.5, 未評等)

焦點內容 
1. 晶心科 3Q26 營收季減 24.6%，低於市場共識，主因 2Q26 認列北
美 CSP 大客戶授權案導致基期較高。公司於 2Q26 已將人力精簡至
320 人，使 3Q26 營業費用季減 34.1%，營業損失收斂至 0.1 億
元，每股虧損 0.58 元。展望 4Q26，公司預估 EPS 將轉正，預期費
用將維持 3Q26 水準，並目標未來每年營收將以 20-30%年增成
長。 
2. 管理層預期 AI 為公司最大成長動能，1-3Q26 營收占比已達
53%。北美 CSP 方面，MTIA 第一、二代皆採用公司 IP，預期未來
將持續有更高階產品簽約。此外，公司共有 4 家北美大型客戶，廣
告收入為主的 CSP 已有至少兩代產品規劃中，另包含以網購為主的
CSP 客戶貢獻長期 IoT AI 營收以及手機晶片大廠。 
3. 車用方面，公司目前每年可完成 2-3 顆車規核心認證，並於
10M26 取得歐洲車用晶片客戶 design win。權利金方面， RISC-V
占權利金比重提升至 21%，預期 RISC-V 權利金占比將持續提升，
並與 V3 形成雙引擎動能。 
結論 
晶心科隨北美 CSP AI 專案持續簽約、車用 design win 落地及 RISC-V
權利金逐步發酵，營收結構將持續改善。然而權利金貢獻仍需時間累
積，且公司長期規畫為維持現有研發規模，因此我們仍保守看待公司
營運前景。

3Q26 財報 
 營收：4.3 億元，季減 24.6%，年減 8.4% 
 授權金 2.4 億元，季減 41.9%，年減 28.2%，主因 2Q26 認列
北美 CSP 大客戶授權案致基期較高 
 權利金 0.9 億元，季增 23.0%，年增 21.1% 
 營業費用：4.4 億元，季減 34.1%，年減 10.0% 
 研發費用 2.8 億元，2Q26 進行人員與產品線調整，人力由 500
人降至約 320 人 
 公司將以 3Q26 扣除員工酬勞後的月平均費用作為後續基準 
 營業利益率：-3.2% 
 淨損：0.3 億元，2Q26 淨損 0.7 億元，3Q25 淨利 0.1 億元 
 LPS：0.58 元 
 對比 2Q26 LPS 為 1.34 元，3Q25 EPS 為 0.17 元 
 7M26-8M26 因員工酬勞費用增加小幅虧損，9M26 單月 EPS 轉
正

### Page 2

台灣 晶心科

Q&A 
 北美 CSP 採用 RISC-V 的意願，以及未來 1-2 年 AI 加速器專案的競
爭優勢與能見度？ 
 主要客戶為 Meta，總計有 4 家北美大型客戶 
 以廣告收入為主的 CSP 將 TPU 導入手機晶片，目前至少兩代產
品規劃中；以網購為主的 CSP 則為長期 IoT AI 客戶 
 手機晶片大廠主要應用於 Wi-Fi 及 Bluetooth；美國自駕計程車
客戶前後代晶片皆採用 
 RISC-V 鼓勵客戶將 domain knowledge 轉為客製指令，以較低成
本實現硬體加速，公司認為此為與 ARM 的競爭優勢 
 車用電子及 Edge AI 的洽談與驗證進度為何，能否成為 2027-28 年
成長引擎？ 
 過去每年公司約取得 1 顆車規核心認證，目前具備每年 2-3 顆的
能力 
 車用市場需長期耕耘，2027-28 年將有貢獻 
 10M26 已取得歐洲車用晶片客戶 Design win ，並打入車規晶片
供應鏈 
 2027 年以後授權金與權利金的結構變化？ 
 RISC-V 自 2021 年即貢獻權利金，進展較快 
 預期 RISC-V 權利金將持續提升，V3 權利金則有長尾效應形成雙
引擎 
 1-3Q26 維護費已超過 2025 全年，彌補權利金成長不及授權
金。同時，公司對授權金與維護費正向看待 
 AI XPU 的 host CPU 方向？效能與 ARM 相比如何？ 
 其可分為運算加速與管理控制兩系列，公司為首家推出 RISC-V 
vector 指令集的廠商 
 Meta 單顆晶片共搭載 129 顆晶心科 CPU 核心 
 Meta 為何選擇 RISC-V？合作的收費模式？ 
 Meta 重視 RISC-V 可將經驗轉為客製指令，且其產品涵蓋
inference 及 training 
 Edge AI 的策略與潛在客戶？ 
 無人機、機器人及自駕車已有成功案例；公司認為小型語言模型
(SLM)將推動 LLM 進入邊緣裝置，適合 RISC-V 發展 
 中國已有客戶以 N45 開發超過 100 種工業電腦模組，應用於機
器人關節

 人力由 500 人降至約 320 人，原因包括關閉德州設計中心、精
簡冗員，以及導入 Agentic AI 工具使部分工程師生產力提升至 5

則不易裁員

未認列，主因客製化案開發時間較長 
 權利金比重何時會提升？ 
 公司認為權利金占比應隨客戶量產自然提升

### Page 3

3
審慎樂觀．均衡配置．聚焦AI與獲利成長
投
資
策
略
市場展望投資策略
 股債並進、保留部分現金部位 : 全球經濟維持溫和成長，
美國利率可能維持較高水準，但企業獲利仍受AI投資循
環帶動而持續改善。建議採取均衡配置策略，保留適度
流動性，逢震盪布局受惠AI基礎建設、半導體、高品質
科技及原物料等長期趨勢產業。
 配置較高信評債，信用利差偏低不追價：高利率環境下，
投等債仍提供具吸引力收益，並具分散風險效果。非投
等債受惠企業基本面穩健及經濟韌性，但信用利差處於
低位，投資策略著重選債品質與產業分散，留意未來再
融資風險帶來的信用分化。(Page 16、18)
 科技股與原物料同步看好，掌握AI第二波受惠機會：AI
投資主題從GPU與半導體擴展至電力基建、儲能及能源
供應鏈。資料中心耗電增加，電網升級、變壓器、銅材、
電纜及儲能需求提升，看好AI科技股及受惠電力資本支
出循環的原物料與工業股。(詳見Page 16、18)
 匯市展望：美國經濟韌性與相對較高利率水準仍支撐美
元，但財政赤字擴大，美元中長期升值動能可能趨緩。

短期受惠避險需求與利差優勢，美元將呈偏強格局。美
元指數要持穩在102.00上方，取決於美國數據。(詳見
Page 8、9)
 債市展望：美國企業違約率未惡化，AI投資與企業現金
流改善支撐信用基本面。非投等債存續期間較短、對利
率敏感度較低，在高收益率環境下具收益配置價值。需
留意未來再融資壓力帶來的信用風險分化。
 股市展望 : 美股收歷史新高，市場看好AI資本支出不會
因債券收益率上升而收手。資本支出成美股和經濟的重
要驅動力。若關鍵公司給出樂觀指引，且債市回穩，有
望推動美股在年底強勢收官。(詳見Page 14、15)

### Page 4

非農爆冷，升息降溫，美債跌出機會
4
美國失業率仍處歷史低位 數據解讀
➢ 美國失業率處歷史低位，Fed考慮再次升息時，仍將聚
焦在通膨。利率期貨市場大幅降低10月升息的押注，非
農數據公布後，利率期貨市場對10月的升息機率一度不
到10%。
➢ 這是11月中期選舉前最後一份就業報告，預計經濟狀況
將成為影響美國選民投票的重要因素。勞動力市場是其
中的一部分，但盡管消費者仍在持續支出，人們對生活
成本揮之不去的擔憂仍是關注焦點。
➢ 截至8月底，今年共有6,250億美元資金淨流入美國債券
共同基金和ETF，為2010年統計以來同期最高水準。隨
著投資人調整資產配置、或將部分資金從股票轉向固定
收益產品，流入速度也將加快。

### Page 5

聯準會轉向技術性擴表，流動性有撐
5
銀行存款準備金在2.9至3.1兆美元間波動
數據解讀
➢ 聯準會的資產負債表自2025年底重啟購債後，雖有短期
回升，但若從整體趨勢來看，目前的規模相較於2022年
的高峰，是呈現大幅縮減（QT）。即使如此，目前資產
總額仍遠高於2008年金融海嘯前不到 1兆美元的水準。
➢ 銀行存款準備金餘額（Reserve Balances with Federal
Reserve Banks）整體在2.9至3.1兆美元間波動，反映
美國銀行體系流動性處充足水準，同時聯準會採取了放
寬部分大型銀行資本要求的政策。準備金增加代表金融
體系流動性充裕，有利股市、信用債與風險資產表現。
➢ Fed雖已結束QT最激進階段，但未重回QE，未來兩年或
進入「被動擴表、主動維持3兆美元準備金」的新常態，
對美債、信用債與AI科技股皆形成溫和流動性支持。
Fed資產負債表未來的擴張路徑

➢ 歐元自2025年5月以來首次跌破1.13，對法國政府債務
的擔憂加劇歐元拋售壓力。德國、法國、義大利和西班
牙9月通膨數據偏熱，ECB繼續專注“將利率恢復至略
具限制性的區間”，預計歐洲12月再升息25bps。
➢ 日圓維持弱勢震盪，日圓因美日收益率差距擴大而承壓，
受美日利差持續擴大、中東衝突帶來的避險需求流入美
元以及日本央行升息態度影響。
➢ 市場權衡中東流量復甦與美伊朗談判僵局，原油價格下
跌。荷姆茲海峽流量升至每日1,320萬桶。黃金下跌，
接近4,183美元，因美國國債收益率走高，不過，在全
球債券拋售之際，黃金吸引避險需求，並守住陣地。

➢ 市場對法國高額政府債務及2027年大選前的政治不確
定性感到擔憂，導致法國信用違約交換（CDS）大幅

➢ 能源價格波動推升歐元區9月通膨率至3.8%（3年新
高），加劇歐洲央行在應對經濟成長與通膨間的兩難，
削弱歐元吸引力。歐元區面臨壓力，加上市場避險需
求升溫，資金持續流向被視為安全資產的瑞郎。
➢ 近期瑞郎雖曾短暫回落，但隨後迅速收復大部分跌幅，
市場對瑞郎的需求依然強勁。投資人逐漸將避險配置
從歐元轉向瑞郎，EUR/CHF一個月期風險逆轉指標挫
至負值深處（例如一個月期指標跌至-1.25，創下近年
新低），這反映出市場大舉買入歐元賣權（EUR Put，
即看跌歐元/看漲瑞郎），使之溢價遠超歐元買權。

### Page 10

美國信用市場展望仍樂觀
10
債
券
市
場
資料來源：Bloomberg，09/21/2026
圖表解讀
➢ 未來信用市場將呈現更明顯的分化，主要風險包括：
低評級企業再融資壓力上升利息負擔增加、基本面差
異擴大，違約風險逐漸分化。
➢ 利率市場已在反映Fed可能再升息，通膨黏著仍在終端
利率上修，信用市場卻仍反映軟著陸情境，違約率受
控及流動性充裕，因此出現利率市場悲觀，但信用市
場樂觀。
➢ 高利率環境下，再融資風險正逐漸取代違約風險，成
為未來2至3年信用市場最值得關注的潛在壓力來源，
而CCC級企業及私募信貸將是較脆弱的區域。但尚未
形成系統性風險，原因在經濟尚未衰退，美國就業市
場仍穩健，企業獲利尚佳，違約率因此未大幅上升。
IG及HY利差預估
信用市場違約率維持低檔

### Page 11

科技債拉長存續期，整體投等債未惡化
11
科技投等債到期年限維持在約11~11.5年
圖表解讀
➢ 科技債拉長存續期，但整體投資級債存續未明顯惡化，
市場對長天期供給的擔憂可能被高估，科技投等債平
均到期年限自2024年開始維持在約11~11.5年，高於
整體IG指數。整體投等債平均到期年限目前約10.3年，
反而低於2020~2021年高峰約12.3年。
➢ 市場擔憂AI基建、資料中心投資熱潮，微軟、亞馬遜、
Alphabet、Meta等大型科技公司頻繁發債，長天期公
司債供給增加可能導致利差擴大與存續期風險上升。
➢ 2026年投資級債發行量創歷史新高，AI資本支出推升
科技債發行，但大量債務尚未到期，使淨供給增幅相
對溫和。科技公司幾乎沒有債務到期壓力，從表中可
發現，Hyperscalers Gross = 1,570億，Net = 1,450
億差距僅120億美元，代表幾乎沒有舊債到期需要償還。
因此新增發行大部分直接轉化為市場新增供給。
資料來源：Bloomberg，9/24/2026

理區間。關注風險在美國長天期公債殖利率維持高
檔、能源價格波動推升通膨壓力以及地緣政治與財
政赤字議題，可能壓抑估值擴張。

AI相關產業貢獻了今年近50%的EPS成長。未來獲
利成長將由2026年的異常高增速放緩至約11%。

### Page 15

算力容量至2028年將翻四倍
15
圖表解讀
➢ 資本支出增速放緩，算力規模仍持續擴張。預計
Hyperscalers總算力容量將從2025年的約36GW增
至2028年的約144GW，接近擴大四倍。谷歌將成
為新增容量最多的公司，2027年和2028年分別新增
約9GW和11GW，主要用於訓練Gemini、推動GCP
增長及支持Search和YouTube的生成式AI功能。
➢ 算力結構也在變化，預計ASIC在新增算力中的占比
將從2025年的34%，升至2028年的66%，谷歌TPU
和亞馬遜Trainium將成為主要驅動力。AI算力需求
仍在增長，但行業從“搶GPU”轉向“提升單位算
力效率”。輝達依然占據核心地位，但雲廠商自研
晶片的重要性持續上升。
➢ 對投資而言，不只要看AI今天創造多少營收，更要
看 AI未來會占企業 IT／資本支出的比例。如果AI企
業採用曲線複製Cloud當年的模式，AI CapEx、
GPU、資料中心、電力、網路、半導體的長期需求
可能遠比目前市場看到的更大。

### Page 16

資料中心 + 電力基建，工業股看漲
16
圖表解讀
➢ 美國總營建支出自2006年房地產泡沫高峰約1.21兆
美元，跌至金融海嘯後的約0.8兆美元，疫情後快速
擴張，今年8月達2.20兆美元，比2006年房市榮景
高出約80%，顯示美國固定資產投資規模已進入歷
史新高區間。
➢ 營建支出表面仍創歷史新高，但結構已出現分化。
住宅景氣降溫，製造業建廠高峰已過，商業建築疲
弱，但AI資料中心年增73%，電力基建維持強勁成
長， 非住宅投資由AI資本支出獨撐，
➢ 美國固定投資循環的核心已從「房地產與製造業」，
正式轉向「AI資料中心 + 電力基礎建設」，這也是
美國及台灣、南韓等AI供應鏈未來1至2年獲利持續
上修的重要支撐，也是推動工業股看漲核心動能。

### Page 20

台灣是全球AI光互連供應鏈重要樞紐
20
圖表解讀
➢ 台灣憑藉完整半導體與先進封裝聚落，已成為全球
AI光互連與矽光子供應鏈的核心樞紐，產業聚焦光
收發模組、光元件、矽光子高速交換器、網通設備。
➢ 生成式AI需求，2030年全球AI token月用量將達
120 Quadrillion(12京)，是2026年的24倍，但面臨
I/O牆的挑戰，AI晶片算力每兩年成長3倍，記憶體
頻寬只成長1.6倍，晶片間傳輸資料的I/O頻寬只成
長1.4倍，銅導線在高頻環境下已逼近長距離傳輸極
限，必須將傳輸從銅過渡到光，延伸訊號傳輸距離，
使AI資料中心實現scale-up及scale-out的架構設計。
➢ 台積電前段光子積體電路（PIC）製程代工與後段先
進封裝垂直整合，引爆AI資料中心的矽光子與共同
封裝光學（CPO）量產革命。台廠受惠族群包括
第一梯隊：光元件封裝，聯鈞（3450）
第二梯隊：PIC與矽光子供應鏈，上詮（3363）
第三梯隊：先進封裝與設備日月光（3711）
*什麼是 I/O Wall？若GPU是超跑：算力 =引擎馬力I/O =高速公路
